Triggers when you reach for a default/fallback (`?? `, `|| []`, `.get(k, FB)`), a skip (`if not present: continue`), a swallow (`try/except: pass`, `|| true`, `2&gt;/dev/null`), a cast/escape-hatch to make types fit, a retry/sleep to get past a flake, or any 'make it work no matter what' edit. - **failure-domain-log**: Use when a refusal fires, a bug recurs, the agent makes a mistake the user has to correct twice, or the user explicitly invokes /failure-log.
